Neutral skeptical reviewer. /root/paper/foyer. READ-ONLY. CCF-B / CAS Q2 target.

CONTEXT: negative-result measurement paper. The authors just unified two things and changed many
numbers as a result: (a) the main-trace reuse accounting now takes prompt and uncached counts from
the same source (steps.jsonl) instead of mixing metrics.csv with steps.jsonl; (b) derived quantities
now use full-precision inputs (frontier anchors, running means, realization fractions). Every
realization percentage and every slope in the paper moved slightly.

SCOPE -- only these three checks, be mechanical:

(1) SAME NAME, DIFFERENT MEANING. The paper has several values that appear as the same numeral in
different senses. Three the authors nearly confused today: "36.3" is BOTH the device-only
realization floor (now 36.4) AND cap1's idle share (legitimately 36.3); "92.6" is realization as a
share of the trace BOUND while the same run's reuse as a share of its PROMPT is 69.9; "0.55" appears
as a delta and elsewhere. Enumerate every numeral in the paper that appears with two or more
distinct meanings, say which is which, and flag any place where the text does not disambiguate.

(2) RECOMPUTE THE CHANGED NUMBERS from the primary sources. All realization percentages
(paper/data/reuse.csv, reuse_x.csv), all slopes (slope.csv), the host-tier ranges, the idle ranges
(idle_pooled.csv, decomp.csv), and Table I's deltas (table1.csv). Report MATCH or MISMATCH.
For MISMATCH include the recomputed value.

(3) DOES ANY CLAIM DEPEND ON A NUMBER THAT MOVED? The realization floor went 36.3 -> 36.4, the
device-only midpoints moved, cap3_fixed went 78.9 -> 79.0, the anchors moved. Check whether any
QUALITATIVE claim in the paper (a verdict, an ordering, a "holds/does not hold") would change if the
old values were right, i.e. whether anything is now resting on a rounding-level difference.

OUTPUT: ## (1) ... ## (2) ... ## (3) ... then ## Acceptance assessment with a rough probability
range and what it rests on. A judgement, not a prediction. Cite file:line. Do not invent scores.

# 前缀复用：trace 定的上界 vs 配置兑现了多少 —— 由 scripts/lab/gen_reuse.py 生成，勿手改
#
# trace 上界（独立于观测，从 replay_pois200_l04.csv 求和）:
#   200 会话 999 步；sum(prefix_len) = 21540371 tokens；prompt 总量 = 28526871 tokens
#   => 计划复用率 ceiling_frac = 0.7551（这就是论文里那个 75% 的来源）
#   上界【必须】这样定义。曾经写成「数据里最多复用的量就是天花板」，是循环论证。
#
# realized_frac = reuse_tok / trace 上界 —— 这一个数才是「兑现了多少」。
#   device-only 随 R̄ 一路掉；host tier 在控制器运行区间内守住。
#   注意【没有任何配置兑现到 1.0】：实测最好也只有 0.9255。
#
# 比例一律由未舍入的 *_tok 列算出。曾经拿舍入到 0.1M 的列算，cap1 印成 69.8%%（真值 69.9%%）。
# contested: 该 run 是否落在主机争用窗口（见 concurrency.csv）。三档含义：
#   clean     = 生命周期内同机 <3 条，未受影响
#   watch     = 同机 >=3 条持续 >=60 分钟，但实测 tau2 未见整体抬升（cap1 即此档）
#   CONTESTED = 同机 >=4 条持续 >=60 分钟，tau1_ms 这一列不可用；
#               reuse/recompute 是体积量，实测与同族干净 run 一致。

# Foyer — 项目约定

内存受限 GPU 上 agentic LLM serving 的准入控制研究。论文在 `paper/`（IEEEtran + LaTeX），
实验在共享的 2×RTX 3090 上跑，原始日志在实验机 `lab-3090:/data/xbw/turnstile/results/`。

## 证据纪律（所有工作都适用）

- **不许编造**引用、实验数字、数据集、结果。数字对不上就报对不上，不要顺手改圆。
- **论文里出现的每个数字都要能追到**代码、日志或 `paper/data/*.csv`。追不到的就标出来，
  不要留在正文里。
- **改 LaTeX 不许改科学含义**。措辞归措辞，结论归结论，两者分开说。
- 下结论前先看文件。涉及实验的陈述必须来自 `results/`、`paper/data/` 或实验机日志。

## 2026-09-24 新增的四条纪律（都是一天内犯的）

1. **不要把【还没跑】的实验写成已跑。** 一天内犯了两次：先在 §III 写
   "§results reports the test"（codex 抓），改完后在摘要、引言、模型节又写了
   五处现在时（子 agent 抓）。两轮审查独立命中同一处，说明这不是笔误是习惯。
   规矩：引用一个实验之前，**先去 results/ 或 paper/data/ 里确认它落地了**；
   没落地就写「已排队」并注明落地后再报。这是本项目第一条纪律
   （不许编造实验数字）的变体，而它比编造更隐蔽 —— 编造是假数，这是真计划。

2. **两条管线算同一个量，必须共用定义。** 表 I 的 Δ 来自 `gen_table.py`（读
   runs.csv，1 位小数），§V 的 Δ 来自 `frontier.py`（读原始日志，全精度）——
   同一条臂两边给 −0.55 和 −0.57。修法是让 `gen_table.py` **import**
   `frontier.py` 的 `FRONT` 与 `front_slo`，而不是各自实现一遍。
   **推论：任何「两个脚本都算过同一个数」的地方，都要检查它们是不是共用定义。**

3. **生成器的 run 列表不全会静默丢数据。** `gen_eviction.py` 的列表里有
   `foyer_hz1`，但本地镜像缺那条 run，于是重跑生成器时它无声消失 ——
   数据文件的集合与正文引用的集合不一致，而且没有任何报错。
   现在缺 run 会显式打印。**推论：凡是从「一批 run」生成表的脚本，都要对缺失
   报错，不能 continue 了事。**

4. **判据写在正文里，代码里也必须实现。** §V 的归因判据写的是「在跑数【相当】
   但步成本更高」，代码的 `elif` 只查了 τ，没查可比性 —— 于是任何跑得更多的臂
   都会被归成「步成本」，而那只是 τ 随 R̄ 的常规趋势。这条 bug 让归因表多算了
   五条。**推论：正文里每一条带阈值的判据，都要能在生成脚本里指出对应的那一行。**

## 这个项目的三条血的教训

1. **手算的表一定会错。** `paper/data/decomp.csv` 曾有一行 busy/idle 来自另一条 run、
   wall 却是对的，两个数自相矛盾，图 2 因此画错，几个月没人发现。任何汇总表都应由
   `scripts/lab/*.py` 生成并带断言（见 `gen_decomp.py`：busy+idle 必须精确等于采样跨度，
   残差非零直接退出）。
1b. **从错的列抄数 —— 已经犯过四次。** 手抄汇总表时把相邻一列的数当成目标列：
   `pairB_cap2hc` 的 busy/idle 来自另一条 run、`foyer_hz2`/`foyer_hz0` 的 running
   抄错、表 I 的 rg4/foyfix 两行 Δ 用了中途读数、相关工作的「round-1+ 命中率
   89.8%」其实是 foyer_hc 的 **SLO**（真值 80.9%）。
   **凡是从表里抄一个数，抄完回头看一眼它在原表里是哪一列。**

2. **中位数会骗人。** `tool_wait` 中位 0.35 s 让整个项目以为会话不离开引擎；均值是 16.85 s。
   报分布的时候，中位、均值、p90、最大值一起报。
3. **"还没到" 和 "坏了" 长得一样。** 曾三次把「trace 的第一个会话还没到」误判成「实验卡住」。
   判断一个 run 是否正常，先看 `runner.out` 和它已跑时长，别只看 steps=0。

## 撤回记录

已经撤回的主张不许复活，除非有新证据：`tool_wait` 0.1s→16.85s、oracle 单调性、
记账重复计费、驱逐→重算推高步时间（被 `num_retracted_reqs≈0` 与
`prompt_tokens_total == 计划 prefill` 反驳）、空转率随并发单调下降。
详见 `paper/claims_audit.md` 与 `docs/claim_register_20260921.md`。

## 引用红线

- arXiv **2608.30830** 自报对 MORI/Continuum 的 head-to-head 胜利**已被否证，禁引**；
  它的 41% goodput 损失与 ~10× 复活差可以引。
- **PrefixShield (2608.01657)** 已占 "admission responsibility" 术语，需避撞。
- 任何引用的标题、作者、年份都必须核实过才能进 `refs.bib`。**不要凭记忆写文献。**

## 硬件与运维

- 平时只用**卡 2 和卡 3**。卡 0/1 仅限 00:00–08:00 且确认空闲（`memory.used < 200 MiB`）。
- **别人占着卡就等，不要算剩余空间够不够。只在完整的卡上跑。**
- **不要同时占满四张卡。** 卡 0/1 可用时，总并发仍应 <= 2 条车道。
  2026-09-21 学到过一次（记在 `scripts/watchdog.sh` 注释里）：把四张卡全占满，
  长跑进程会被共享机器上其它租户的 reaper 杀掉。**2026-09-24 又犯了一次**：
  四条车道一起开，卡 0/1 的两条 run 在 02:36:46 / 02:37:00（相隔 14 秒，
  同一绝对时刻）双双被 SIGTERM，只跑到 679 和 624 步就终止，两条都作废。
  症状：`steps.jsonl` 里 `connection closed before message completed` 在某个时刻
  突然成簇出现，`status=FAILED`；server 日志里 `Subprocess scheduler_0 crashed
  with exit code -15`。**这个 SIGTERM 是结果不是原因，别去查谁发的。**
- **2026-09-24 追加：四条车道就算没被杀，数据也已经废了。** 审计（`scripts/lab/gen_concurrency.py`
  → `paper/data/concurrency.csv`）查明：同机同时 ≥4 条 run 且持续 ≥60 分钟，
  会把**逐请求 decode 间隔中位 τ₂ 抬高 25~40%**（30.9~33.3 ms vs 同族干净值 23.8~25.7）。
  2~3 条不抬（pairB_foyerhc 3 条跑 277 分钟，τ₂=23.8，正常）。
  **危险之处：引擎侧所有指标都看不出异常** —— `num_running_reqs`、占用率、驱逐量、
  重算量在这四条臂上与干净 run 不可区分，只有客户端实测的 τ₂ 露馅。
  四条控制器臂（bres/hz0/hz2/breshz1）因此作废并重跑（新名加 `_r2`）。
  详见 `docs/finding_concurrency_contamination_20260924.md`。
  **判据要按时长不按峰值**：`foyer_hz1` 峰值到过 4 条但只有 14 分钟，τ₂ 正常。
- **跑实验时必须挂 `gpu_watch.py`**（2026-09-24 用户提出，已接入 `run_matrix.py`）：
  池子守卫只管【启动那一刻】，跑到一半别人往同一张卡上挂进程会静默污染这一段的
  tau / SLO / 墙钟，而数据看上去完全正常。watcher 每 15 秒记一次该卡的显存、
  利用率、全部计算进程及其属主，输出到 `<run_dir>/gpu_watch.jsonl`；属主不是自己的
  标成 foreign。汇总用 `gpu_watch_summary.py <rundir>`，有它就可以写"这次测量期间
  该卡独占"。**这是低概率情况的便宜保险，不是对已有结果的质疑**：起跑时的池子守卫
  已保证池子严格对齐，且多次重复之间墙钟 CV 只有 0.9~1.8% —— 真被偷算力不会这么稳。
  所以早期 run 照常可信，不因缺这个记录而作废。
- KV pool 必须落在 `101,432 ± 20`，否则该 run 作废，不记录——SGLang 按启动时的空闲显存
  定池子，池子小了数字就不可比。

## 改文件

- **`open(path,"w")` 会在 write 之前就截断文件。** 2026-09-24 犯过：`s.replace(...)`
  后面多打一个逗号使返回值成了元组，write 抛 TypeError，但文件已经被清空
  （`sec_discussion.tex` 整篇丢失，页数从 15 掉到 12 是这个信号）。
  规矩：写完先断言 `isinstance(s, str)`，或者先写临时文件再 `os.replace`。
  征兆是**页数突然变少** —— 编译后页数掉了，先怀疑有文件被清空。
- 长文档改完后 `git diff --stat` 看一眼增删行数，与预期差太多就停下查。

## 语言

- 对用户回复一律用**中文**。代码、路径、报错原文可保留英文。
- TUI 里不要写 LaTeX 公式，用纯文本或中文名。

## git

commit message 里含反引号时**不要用 `-m`**，走 `-F 文件`——已因此静默丢词三次。

     1	#!/usr/bin/env python3
     2	"""跨 trace 的复用兑现表 —— paper/data/reuse_x.csv（论文 §VI 的数字来源）。
     3	
     4	为什么单独一个表：gen_reuse.py 只覆盖主 trace 的 13 条 run（它读的 eviction.csv
     5	是 poisons200 专用）。§VI 引用了第二条 trace 的兑现率（74.8%、67.5%），那两个数
     6	一度只存在于正文里、不在任何 CSV —— 违反本项目「每个数字都要能追到」的纪律。
     7	这个脚本让它们有出处。
     8	
     9	定义与 gen_reuse.py 逐字一致：
    10	  上界 = sum(prefix_len) / sum(prefix_len + input_len)，从 trace 文件直接求和，
    11	         独立于任何观测（runner 的 summary.json 也报同一个数，本脚本断言二者相符）
    12	  实测复用 = prompt - uncached（steps.jsonl 的 server_uncached_prompt_tokens 累加）
    13	  兑现率 = 实测复用 / 上界 —— 「兑现了多少」这个数
    14	
    15	用法: python3 gen_reuse_x.py <trace.csv> <results_dir> <out.csv> <run> [<run> ...]
    16	"""
    17	import csv, json, os, statistics, sys
    18	
    19	
    20	def trace_bound(path):
    21	    rows = list(csv.DictReader(open(path)))
    22	    pre = sum(int(r["prefix_len"]) for r in rows)
    23	    tot = sum(int(r["prefix_len"]) + int(r["input_len"]) for r in rows)
    24	    return pre, tot, pre / tot, len({r["session_id"] for r in rows}), len(rows)
    25	
    26	
    27	def main():
    28	    trace, root, out_p = sys.argv[1], sys.argv[2], sys.argv[3]
    29	    runs = sys.argv[4:]
    30	    pre, tot, bound, nsess, nsteps = trace_bound(trace)
    31	
    32	    rows, bad = [], []
    33	    for r in runs:
    34	        d = os.path.join(root, r)
    35	        sp, mp, sj = (os.path.join(d, f) for f in ("steps.jsonl", "metrics.csv", "summary.json"))
    36	        if not (os.path.exists(sp) and os.path.exists(sj)):
    37	            bad.append((r, "缺 steps.jsonl 或 summary.json"))
    38	            continue
    39	        steps = [json.loads(l) for l in open(sp) if l.strip()]
    40	        prompt = sum(s.get("prompt_len") or 0 for s in steps)
    41	        unc = sum(s.get("server_uncached_prompt_tokens") or 0 for s in steps)
    42	        reuse = prompt - unc
    43	
    44	        # 断言 1：本脚本从 trace 求和得到的上界，必须等于 runner 自己报的
    45	        # planned_prefix_hit_rate —— 两个独立来源，对不上说明口径搞错了
    46	        sj_d = json.load(open(sj)).get("replay", {})
    47	        if "planned_prefix_hit_rate" in sj_d:
    48	            assert abs(sj_d["planned_prefix_hit_rate"] - bound) < 1e-4, \
    49	                "%s: 本脚本算的上界 %.4f != runner 报的 %.4f" % (r, bound, sj_d["planned_prefix_hit_rate"])
    50	        # 断言 2：实测复用不得超过上界
    51	        assert reuse <= pre, "%s: 复用 %d 超过上界 %d" % (r, reuse, pre)
    52	
    53	        rn = [float(x["sglang:num_running_reqs"]) for x in csv.DictReader(open(mp))
    54	              if x.get("sglang:num_running_reqs")]
    55	        occ = [float(x["sglang:token_usage"]) for x in csv.DictReader(open(mp))
    56	               if x.get("sglang:token_usage")]
    57	        busy = [v for v in rn if v > 0]
    58	        rows.append({
    59	            "run": r,
    60	            "sessions": nsess, "steps": len(steps),
    61	            "Rbar_busy": "%.6f" % (statistics.mean(busy) if busy else 0),
    62	            "occ_p50_pct": "%.1f" % (statistics.median(occ) * 100 if occ else 0),
    63	            "prompt_tok": prompt,
    64	            "reuse_tok": reuse,
    65	            "recompute_tok": unc,
    66	            "reuse_frac": "%.4f" % (reuse / prompt) if prompt else "",
    67	            "bound_frac": "%.4f" % bound,
    68	            "realized_frac": "%.6f" % (reuse / pre),
    69	        })
    70	
    71	    rows.sort(key=lambda x: float(x["Rbar_busy"]))
    72	    header = "\n".join([
    73	        "# 跨 trace 的复用兑现 —— 由 scripts/lab/gen_reuse_x.py 生成，勿手改",
    74	        "# trace: %s（%d 会话 %d 步）" % (os.path.basename(trace), nsess, nsteps),
    75	        "# 上界 = %d / %d = %.4f，从 trace 的 prefix_len 求和，独立于观测；" % (pre, tot, bound),
    76	        "#   与 runner summary.json 的 planned_prefix_hit_rate 断言相符。",
    77	        "# 实测复用 = prompt - uncached（steps.jsonl），兑现率 = 实测 / 上界。",
    78	        "# 用途：§VI 在第二条 trace 上重算同一组量。",
    79	        "#   主 trace 的同一组量在 paper/data/reuse.csv（gen_reuse.py 生成）；两张表",
    80	        "#   用同一套定义，合起来才是 §VI 那张跨 trace 对照。",
    81	        "# 注意【兑现率跨 trace 不可用占用率概括】：",
    82	        "#   这条 trace 在占用 15.8% 时兑现 0.7478，而主 trace 在占用 22.4% 时兑现 0.9255。",
    83	        "run,sessions,steps,Rbar_busy,occ_p50_pct,prompt_tok,reuse_tok,recompute_tok,reuse_frac,bound_frac,realized_frac"])
    84	    fields = ["run", "sessions", "steps", "Rbar_busy", "occ_p50_pct", "prompt_tok",
    85	              "reuse_tok", "recompute_tok", "reuse_frac", "bound_frac", "realized_frac"]
    86	    body = header + "\n" + "\n".join(",".join(str(r[k]) for k in fields) for r in rows) + "\n"
    87	    assert isinstance(body, str)
    88	    with open(out_p, "w") as fh:
    89	        fh.write(body)
    90	
    91	    print("trace 上界 %.4f；写入 %s：%d 行" % (bound, out_p, len(rows)))
    92	    for r in rows:
    93	        print("  %-16s R̄ %-6s 占用 %5s%%  兑现 %s" % (
    94	            r["run"], r["Rbar_busy"], r["occ_p50_pct"], r["realized_frac"]))
    95	    if bad:
    96	        print("!! 未写入：")
    97	        for n, why in bad:
    98	            print("   %-16s %s" % (n, why))
    99	
   100	
   101	if __name__ == "__main__":
   102	    main()
     1	#!/usr/bin/env python3
     2	"""兑现率的逐段斜率 —— paper/data/slope.csv（论文 §VI 的头条数字来源）。
     3	
     4	§VI 的核心对比是「两条 trace 的坍塌形状不同」：
     5	  realhr    -31.3  -36.6  -28.4        基本恒定 —— R̄ 1.41 时还没到悬崖
     6	  主 trace  -10.8  -41.0  -157.4  -126.7    R̄ 1.53 后斜率爆炸
     7	这五个数是论文里第一次出现"斜率"这个口径，此前是当场算的、不在任何 CSV 里。
     8	
     9	斜率定义：相邻两点的兑现率之差 / 运行数之差，单位是「百分点每单位 R̄」。
    10	
    11	用法: python3 gen_slope.py <reuse.csv> <reuse_x.csv> <out.csv>
    12	"""
    13	import csv, sys
    14	
    15	
    16	def load(p):
    17	    return [r for r in csv.DictReader(l for l in open(p) if not l.startswith("#"))]
    18	
    19	
    20	def main():
    21	    src = [(("main", r["run"]), float(r["Rbar_busy"]), float(r["realized_frac"]))
    22	           for r in load(sys.argv[1]) if r["family"] == "device_only"]
    23	    # 只取【静态点】——控制器臂不参与前沿/序列，混进来会把两段连成一段
    24	    # （第一版没滤，把 realhr_foyer 也算进去，于是 realhr 变成 3 段而不是 4 段）
    25	    src += [(("xtrace", r["run"]), float(r["Rbar_busy"]), float(r["realized_frac"]))
    26	            for r in load(sys.argv[2]) if "_cap" in r["run"]]
    27	    by = {}
    28	    for (tr, run), r, f in src:
    29	        by.setdefault(tr, []).append((r, f, run))
    30	    rows = []
    31	    for tr, pts in by.items():
    32	        pts.sort()
    33	        for (r0, f0, n0), (r1, f1, n1) in zip(pts, pts[1:]):
    34	            rows.append({
    35	                "trace": tr, "from_run": n0, "to_run": n1,
    36	                "r0": "%.3f" % r0, "r1": "%.3f" % r1,
    37	                "realized0_pct": "%.1f" % (f0 * 100), "realized1_pct": "%.1f" % (f1 * 100),
    38	                "slope_pp_per_unit": "%.1f" % ((f1 - f0) * 100 / (r1 - r0)),
    39	            })
    40	    # 全范围斜率单独出两行：正文用它与另一条 trace 比"整体陡不陡"。
    41	    # ⚠ 必须全范围对全范围 —— 曾经拿 realhr 的【三点】斜率(-34.5)去比主 trace 的
    42	    # 全范围(-66.8)，区间不可比，而且 realhr 自己的全范围其实是 -32.3。
    43	    for tr, pts in by.items():
    44	        pts.sort()
    45	        r0, f0, n0 = pts[0]; r1, f1, n1 = pts[-1]

    47	                     "r0": "%.3f" % r0, "r1": "%.3f" % r1,
    48	                     "realized0_pct": "%.1f" % (f0 * 100),
    49	                     "realized1_pct": "%.1f" % (f1 * 100),
    50	                     "slope_pp_per_unit": "%.2f" % ((f1 - f0) * 100 / (r1 - r0))})
    51	    rows.sort(key=lambda x: (x["trace"], float(x["r0"]), float(x["r1"])))
    52	    header = "\n".join([
    53	        "# 兑现率的逐段斜率 —— 由 scripts/lab/gen_slope.py 生成，勿手改",

    59	        "trace,from_run,to_run,r0,r1,realized0_pct,realized1_pct,slope_pp_per_unit"])
    60	    fields = ["trace", "from_run", "to_run", "r0", "r1", "realized0_pct",
    61	              "realized1_pct", "slope_pp_per_unit"]
    62	    body = header + "\n" + "\n".join(",".join(str(x[k]) for k in fields) for x in rows) + "\n"
    63	    assert isinstance(body, str)
    64	    with open(sys.argv[3], "w") as fh:
    65	        fh.write(body)
    66	    print("写入 %s：%d 行" % (sys.argv[3], len(rows)))
    67	    for x in rows:
    68	        print("  %-7s R̄ %s->%s  兑现 %5s->%5s  斜率 %s" % (
    69	            x["trace"], x["r0"], x["r1"], x["realized0_pct"], x["realized1_pct"],
    70	            x["slope_pp_per_unit"]))
    71	
    72	
    73	if __name__ == "__main__":
    74	    main()

     2	"""从原始日志重新生成 paper/data/decomp.csv —— 论文 C1/C2/C3 的唯一数据来源。
     3	
     4	为什么要有这个脚本：这张表原来是在终端里手算然后粘进 CSV 的，结果
     5	`pairB_cap2hc` 那一行的 busy/idle 被写成了另一条 run 的值（总长 220.9 分），
     6	而 wall 填的是正确的 295.0 —— 两个数自相矛盾，图 2 因此画出一根短得离谱的
     7	柱子，标签飘在右边 80 分钟处。手算的表没有残差检查，所以这种错误不会被发现。
     8	
     9	本脚本对每条 run 做三件事，并且**强制**检查：
    10	  1. busy + idle 必须精确等于采样跨度 span（残差 != 0 直接报错退出）
    11	  2. span 与 run_summary 的 wall 之差必须落在 [0, 12] 分钟（采样边界造成的固定偏移）
    12	  3. 任何一步失败就把该 run 标成 INVALID，不写入 CSV
    13	
    14	用法:  python3 gen_decomp.py <results_dir> <out.csv> [run ...]
    15	"""
    16	import csv, json, os, statistics, sys, glob
    17	
    18	HEADER = ["run", "wall_min", "span_min", "idle_min", "busy_min", "idle_pct",
    19	          "running", "running_all", "step_back_ms", "step_perreq_ms",
    20	          # τ₁/τ₂ 的超出比例 —— §III 引用「τ₁ 比 τ₂ 高 7.9~39.8%」这个区间。
    21	          # 它是两个导出量之比，此前不落盘（审计报成"找不到源头"）。
    22	          "excess_pct"]
    23	
    24	# 采样周期。metrics.csv 每 5 秒一行。
    25	SAMPLE_S = 5.0
    26	# 采样跨度允许比 run_summary 的墙钟长多少（秒）。采样器比第一个请求早启动、
    27	# 比最后一个请求晚停下，这个偏移在全部 run 上是恒定的 ~6 分钟。
    28	SPAN_SLACK_S = (0.0, 12 * 60)
    29	
    30	
    31	def read_metrics(path):
    32	    """返回 [(ts, num_running_reqs_or_None)]。"""
    33	    rows = []
    34	    with open(path) as f:
    35	        hdr = f.readline().strip().split(",")
    36	        if "sglang:num_running_reqs" not in hdr:
    37	            raise RuntimeError("%s 没有 sglang:num_running_reqs 列" % path)
    38	        i_ts = hdr.index("ts")
    39	        i_rn = hdr.index("sglang:num_running_reqs")
    40	        for line in f:
    41	            c = line.rstrip("\n").split(",")
    42	            if len(c) <= max(i_ts, i_rn):
    43	                continue
    44	            try:
    45	                ts = float(c[i_ts])
    46	            except ValueError:
    47	                continue
    48	            try:
    49	                rn = float(c[i_rn])
    50	            except ValueError:
    51	                rn = None
    52	            rows.append((ts, rn))
    53	    if len(rows) < 10:
    54	        raise RuntimeError("%s 只有 %d 个采样点" % (path, len(rows)))
    55	    return rows
    56	
    57	
    58	def decompose(rows):
    59	    """把相邻样本之间的间隔按【前一个】样本的状态归类。
    60	
    61	    这是论文 C1 的定义。返回分钟数；残差必须为 0。
    62	    """
    63	    span = rows[-1][0] - rows[0][0]
    64	    busy = idle = other = 0.0
    65	    for (t0, r0), (t1, _) in zip(rows, rows[1:]):
    66	        dt = t1 - t0
    67	        if dt <= 0 or dt > SAMPLE_S * 3:      # 采样断档：既不算 busy 也不算 idle
    68	            other += dt
    69	            continue
    70	        if r0 is None:
    71	            other += dt
    72	        elif r0 == 0:
    73	            idle += dt
    74	        else:
    75	            busy += dt
    76	    resid = span - busy - idle - other
    77	    assert abs(resid) < 1e-6, "分解残差 %.6f s != 0" % resid
    78	    assert other == 0.0, "有 %.1f s 采样断档，该 run 的分解不可信" % other
    79	    return span / 60, busy / 60, idle / 60, 100 * idle / span
    80	
    81	
    82	def read_steps(path):
    83	    out = []
    84	    with open(path) as f:
    85	        for line in f:
    86	            line = line.strip()
    87	            if line:

    89	                    out.append(json.loads(line))
    90	                except json.JSONDecodeError:
    91	                    pass
    92	    return out

    94	
    95	def step_perreq_ms(steps):
    96	    """方法二：逐请求 decode 间隔的中位。只取 output_len >= 32 的步。"""
    97	    vals = []
    98	    for s in steps:

   102	        if n >= 32 and tot is not None and ft is not None and n > 1:
   103	            vals.append((tot - ft) / (n - 1))
   104	    return statistics.median(vals) if vals else None
   105	
   106	
   107	def main():

   109	    out_path = sys.argv[2]

   111	    if not runs:
   112	        runs = sorted(os.path.basename(os.path.dirname(p))
   113	                      for p in glob.glob(os.path.join(root, "*", "metrics.csv")))
   114	
   115	    rows, bad = [], []
   116	    for r in runs:
   117	        d = os.path.join(root, r)

   123	
   124	            # 墙钟的口径必须和 run_summary.py 逐字一致，否则图 2 的柱子长度
   125	            # 和图 1 的横坐标就是两个数。见 run_summary.py:91：
   126	            #   墙钟 = 最后一个完成 − 最早一个提交
   127	            # 不能用 st[0]：steps.jsonl 是按完成时间排的。
   128	            subs = [s["submit_timestamp"] for s in steps if s.get("submit_timestamp")]
   129	            comps = [s["complete_timestamp"] for s in steps if s.get("complete_timestamp")]
   130	            if not subs or not comps:
   131	                bad.append((r, "steps.jsonl 缺 submit/complete 时间戳"))
   132	                continue
   133	            wall_min = (max(comps) - min(subs)) / 60.0
   134	
   135	            if not (SPAN_SLACK_S[0] <= (span_min - wall_min) * 60 <= SPAN_SLACK_S[1]):
   136	                bad.append((r, "采样跨度 %.1f 分与墙钟 %.1f 分差 %.1f 分，超出容许的 0-12 分"
   137	                            % (span_min, wall_min, span_min - wall_min)))
   138	                continue
   139	
   140	            running_all = statistics.mean(v for _, v in rows_m if v is not None)
   141	            # τ 的分母必须是【busy 期间的条件均值】，不是全时段均值。
   142	            #
   143	            # 式子 W_busy ≈ G/R̄ · τ 里，R̄ 是「每一步在跑几个请求」；而全时段
   144	            # 采样均值把空转的那些 0 也算进去了，等于 R̄_busy × busy 占比。

   149	            busy_frac = busy_min / span_min if span_min else 1.0

   151	            step_back = (busy_min * 60 * running / tokens * 1000) if tokens else None
   152	            rows.append({
   153	                "run": r.replace("pois200_", ""),
   154	                "wall_min": round(wall_min, 1),
   155	                "span_min": round(span_min, 1),
   156	                "idle_min": round(idle_min, 1),
   157	                "busy_min": round(busy_min, 1),
   158	                "idle_pct": round(idle_pct, 1),
   159	                "running": round(running, 2), "running_all": round(running_all, 2),
   160	                "step_back_ms": round(step_back, 1) if step_back else "",
   161	                "step_perreq_ms": (round(step_perreq_ms(steps), 1)
   162	                                   if step_perreq_ms(steps) else ""),
   163	                # τ₁/τ₂ 超出比例。两个量都非空才算，否则留空。

   170	    if bad:

   175	    with open(out_path, "w", newline="") as f:
   176	        f.write("# 墙钟的两段分解 + 步时间 —— 由 scripts/lab/gen_decomp.py 生成，勿手改\n")
   177	        f.write("# busy/idle: 相邻采样按前一时刻 sglang:num_running_reqs 是否为 0 归类；busy+idle==span（有断言）\n")

     1	#!/usr/bin/env python3
     2	"""空转段的合并统计（全部 vs 排除受争用影响的 run）—— paper/data/idle_pooled.csv。
     3	
     4	为什么要有：论文 §III 引用「1849 段，中位 5.6 s，均值 20.6 s，idle 时间中 69.6%
     5	在 >20 s 的段里」。但那 1849 段里含四条被同机争用影响的 run（争用抬高 τ，
     6	busy 变长、idle 变短，所以段时长【可能】被影响）。本脚本回答：排除它们之后
     7	这些数会变吗？
     8	
     9	答案是不会（中位 5.60→5.00 s，均值 20.65→21.02 s，>20 s 占比 69.6%→70.6%）。
    10	这是因为段时长由【工具等待】决定，是工作负载属性，不是步成本的函数 ——
    11	所以这个检查反过来支持了论文「空转是结构性的」这一主张。
    12	
    13	用法: python3 gen_idle_pooled.py paper/data/idle_seg_raw.csv paper/data/concurrency.csv <out.csv>
    14	"""
    15	import csv, statistics, sys
    16	
    17	BAD = None
    18	
    19	
    20	def load(p):
    21	    return [r for r in csv.DictReader(l for l in open(p) if not l.startswith("#"))]
    22	
    23	
    24	def main():
    25	    raw_p, conc_p, out_p = sys.argv[1:4]
    26	    bad = {r["run"].replace("pois200_", "") for r in load(conc_p)
    27	           if r["verdict"] == "CONTESTED"}
    28	    rows = load(raw_p)
    29	    assert len(rows) > 1000, "原始段只有 %d 条，路径或格式不对" % len(rows)
    30	
    31	    # 四个变体都出：合并在位/均值对【并哪些 run】的敏感性本身就是要报的东西。
    32	    # 实测：中位被 cap1 一条主导（它占 22% 的段，自身中位 5.0 s），去掉它中位
    33	    # 从 5.60 跳到 10.00；而均值（18.3~21.0）与 >20s 占比（64.8~70.6%）稳定。
    34	    damaged = {"foyer_hz2", "foyer_hz0", "foyer_bres"}   # 实测被争用损害的三条
    35	    out = []
    36	    for name, excl in (("all_runs", set()),
    37	                       ("minus_cap1", {"cap1"}),
    38	                       ("minus_damaged3", damaged),
    39	                       ("minus_contested4", bad & {r["run"] for r in rows})):
    40	        sel = [r for r in rows if r["run"] not in excl]
    41	        v = [float(r["duration_s"]) for r in sel]
    42	        tot = sum(v)
    43	        out.append({
    44	            "set": name, "n_segments": len(v), "n_runs": len({r["run"] for r in sel}),
    45	            "median_s": "%.2f" % statistics.median(v),
    46	            "mean_s": "%.2f" % statistics.mean(v),
    47	            "p90_s": "%.2f" % sorted(v)[int(0.9 * len(v))],
    48	            "max_s": "%.1f" % max(v),
    49	            "pct_idle_time_in_gt20s": "%.1f" % (100 * sum(x for x in v if x > 20) / tot),
    50	            "pct_segments_gt20s": "%.1f" % (100 * sum(1 for x in v if x > 20) / len(v)),
    51	        })
    52	
    53	    header = "\n".join([
    54	        "# 空转段的合并统计 —— 由 scripts/lab/gen_idle_pooled.py 生成，勿手改",
    55	        "# clean_only = 排除 concurrency.csv 里判 CONTESTED 的 run（它们的 τ 被同机争用抬高）",
    56	        "# 四个变体：中位与 p90 被 cap1 主导（它占 22% 的段），均值与 >20s 占比稳定。",
    57	        "# 论文引用的「中位 5.6 s」必须写成区间；「均值 ~20 s」与「>20s 占 idle 时间 ~2/3」稳健。",
    58	        "set,n_segments,n_runs,median_s,mean_s,p90_s,max_s,pct_idle_time_in_gt20s,pct_segments_gt20s"])
    59	    fields = ["set", "n_segments", "n_runs", "median_s", "mean_s", "p90_s", "max_s",
    60	              "pct_idle_time_in_gt20s", "pct_segments_gt20s"]
    61	    body = header + "\n" + "\n".join(",".join(str(r[k]) for k in fields) for r in out) + "\n"
    62	    assert isinstance(body, str)
    63	    with open(out_p, "w") as fh:
    64	        fh.write(body)
    65	    print("写入 %s" % out_p)
    66	    for r in out:
    67	        print("  %-11s n=%4d(%2d run) 中位 %5s 均值 %5s  p90 %6s  >20s 占 idle 时间 %5s%%"
    68	              % (r["set"], r["n_segments"], r["n_runs"], r["median_s"], r["mean_s"],
    69	                 r["p90_s"], r["pct_idle_time_in_gt20s"]))
    70	
    71	
    72	if __name__ == "__main__":
    73	    main()

    35	         (527.431963, 94.794795, "cap1")]
    36	
    37	
    38	def front_slo(wall):
    39	    """前沿在给定墙钟处的 SLO 上界（分段线性插值）。超出锚点范围返回 None——
    40	    范围外没有可比的前沿，不能给判决，否则端点外推会凭空造出"越过"。"""
    41	    # 容差：锚点 run 自己的墙钟与 FRONT 里的字面量会差几个 ulp，不加容差会把
    42	    # 锚点自己判成 LEFT-OF-FRONT（2026-09-24 全精度化之后暴露出来的）。
    43	    EPS = 1e-6
    44	    if wall < FRONT[0][0] - EPS or wall > FRONT[-1][0] + EPS:
    45	        return None
    46	    for (w0, s0, _), (w1, s1, _) in zip(FRONT, FRONT[1:]):
    47	        # 容差也要加在这里：只加在上面的范围检查是不够的 —— 锚点 run 自己的墙钟
    48	        # 比 FRONT 里的字面量小 5.7e-8，于是 w0 <= wall 为假、整条循环都不匹配，
    49	        # 锚点被自己的前沿判成 LEFT-OF-FRONT（2026-09-24 全精度化之后暴露的）。
    50	        if w0 - EPS <= wall <= w1 + EPS:
    51	            return s0 + (s1 - s0) * (wall - w0) / (w1 - w0)
    52	    return None
    53	
    54	
    55	def summarise(run):
    56	    p = os.path.join(RESULTS, run, "steps.jsonl")
    57	    if not os.path.exists(p):
    58	        return None
    59	    rows = [json.loads(l) for l in open(p)]
    60	    ts = [r["submit_timestamp"] for r in rows if r.get("submit_timestamp")]
    61	    te = [r["complete_timestamp"] for r in rows if r.get("complete_timestamp")]
    62	    tt = [r["first_token_ms"] for r in rows if r.get("first_token_ms") is not None]
    63	    if not ts or not tt:
    64	        return None
    65	    wall = (max(te) - min(ts)) / 60.0
    66	    slo = 100.0 * sum(1 for x in tt if x <= 10000) / len(tt)
    67	    # 实测并发：按 30 秒采样活跃请求数
    68	    N = 300
    69	    act = []
    70	    for i in range(N):
    71	        t = min(ts) + (max(te) - min(ts)) * i / (N - 1)
    72	        act.append(sum(1 for r in rows if r.get("submit_timestamp") and r.get("complete_timestamp")
    73	                       and r["submit_timestamp"] <= t < r["complete_timestamp"]))
    74	    # 分段 SLO 标准差（十等分）
    75	    seg = []
    76	    for i in range(10):
    77	        s = tt[i * len(tt) // 10:(i + 1) * len(tt) // 10]
    78	        if s:
    79	            seg.append(100.0 * sum(1 for x in s if x <= 10000) / len(s))
    80	    sd = st.pstdev(seg) if len(seg) > 1 else 0.0
    81	    return dict(run=run, steps=len(rows), wall=wall, conc=sum(act) / N, slo=slo, sd=sd)
    82	
    83	
    84	def verdict(d):
    85	    f = front_slo(d["wall"])
    86	    d["front"] = float("nan") if f is None else f
    87	    if f is None:
    88	        d["delta"] = float("nan")
    89	        d["verdict"] = ("LEFT-OF-FRONT (无静态点在更左, 平凡获胜)"
    90	                        if d["wall"] < FRONT[0][0] else "OUT-OF-RANGE (超出现有静态点范围)")
    91	        return d
    92	    d["delta"] = d["slo"] - f
    93	    if d["delta"] > 1.0:
    94	        d["verdict"] = "ABOVE  (越过前沿)"
    95	    elif d["delta"] > -1.0:
    96	        d["verdict"] = "ON     (在线上, 不算赢)"
    97	    else:
    98	        d["verdict"] = "BELOW  (在线下)"
    99	    return d
   100	
   101	
   102	def emit_csv():
   103	    """全精度导出 —— 给 gen_table.py 用。
   104	
   105	    为什么需要：runs.csv 是手抄 run_summary.py 打印的【1 位小数】表得来的，
   106	    而本脚本从原始日志算的是全精度。同一条插值公式，输入 263.3/86.3 与
   107	    263.340318/86.286286 会给出差 0.02pp 的 delta —— 于是表 I 写 -0.55、
   108	    正文写 -0.57（2026-09-24 审稿人抓到的对不上）。让两条管线共用这一个
   109	    全精度来源，而不是各自去读不同精度的表。
   110	    """
   111	    import csv as _csv
   112	    w = _csv.writer(sys.stdout)
   113	    w.writerow(["run", "wall_min", "slo_pct"])
   114	    for nm in sorted(os.listdir(RESULTS)):
   115	        if not nm.startswith("pois200_"):
   116	            continue
   117	        d = summarise(nm)
   118	        if not d or d["steps"] < 999:
   119	            continue
   120	        w.writerow([nm, "%.6f" % d["wall"], "%.6f" % d["slo"]])
   121	
   122	
   123	def main():
   124	    runs = sys.argv[1:]
   125	    if runs == ["--csv"]:
   126	        emit_csv()
   127	        return 0
   128	    if runs == ["--all-foyer"]:
   129	        runs = sorted(r for r in os.listdir(RESULTS)
   130	                      if r.startswith("pois200_foyer") or r.startswith("pois200_cap"))
   131	    out = []
   132	    for r in runs:
   133	        d = summarise(r)
   134	        if d and d["steps"] >= 999:
   135	            out.append(verdict(d))
   136	    print("%-30s %5s %8s %7s %7s %8s %8s  %s" %
   137	          ("run", "步数", "墙钟min", "并发", "SLO%", "前沿SLO", "差(pp)", "判决"))
   138	    for d in sorted(out, key=lambda x: x["wall"]):
   139	        print("%-30s %5d %8.1f %7.2f %7.1f %8.1f %+8.2f  %s" %
   140	              (d["run"], d["steps"], d["wall"], d["conc"], d["slo"], d["front"], d["delta"], d["verdict"]))
   141	    print("\n注意：步数 < 999 的 run 一律不列（未跑完的不能与跑完的比，§多处的教训）。")
   142	
   143	
   144	if __name__ == "__main__":
   145	    main()
     1	#!/usr/bin/env python3
     2	"""从 runs.csv 生成论文表 I 的 LaTeX 表体 —— 让「每个判决都由一个脚本产出」成为真的。
     3	
     4	为什么必须有：表 I 的 Δ 列原来是手抄的，其中两行抄错了（rg4 写成 −24.0 实际
     5	−25.02；foyfix 写成 −48.7 实际 −47.15）。而正文第 §7 声称每个判决都由单一脚本
     6	从原始日志产出 —— 手抄的表让这句话不成立。这个脚本把表变成可复现的。
     7	
     8	判决口径与 scripts/lab/frontier.py 一致：
     9	  包络 = 静态点（含 +HC）的上左包络
    10	  Δ = 该点的 SLO − 包络在同墙钟处的插值 SLO
    11	  判读：Δ > +1.0 越过 / −1.0 ≤ Δ ≤ +1.0 平局 / Δ < −1.0 在线下
    12	
    13	用法: python3 gen_table.py ../paper/data/runs.csv
    14	"""
    15	import csv, os, sys
    16	
    17	# 表里的行标签（与论文一致），以及它们的排序位置由 Δ 决定
    18	LABEL = {
    19	    "default": "no control",
    20	    "cap1": "cap1", "cap2": "cap2", "cap3_fixed": "cap3 (fixed)",
    21	    "cap4": "cap4", "cap5": "cap5",
    22	    "pairB_cap2hc": "cap2+HC", "cap3_hc": "cap3+HC", "cap4_hc": "cap4+HC",
    23	    "cap4_hc_r2": "cap4+HC (repeat)", "cap3_hc_r2": "cap3+HC (repeat)",
    24	    "foyer_hc_r2": "horizon$=$3 (2nd run)", "foyer_sf0_r2": "single-flight off",
    25	    "foyer_hz1": "horizon$=$1",
    26	    "foyer_hz1_r2": "horizon$=$1 (repeat)", "foyer_hc": "horizon$=$3 (base)",
    27	    "foyer_hz2": "horizon$=$2", "foyer_bres": "resident base",
    28	    "foyer_hz0": "horizon$=$0", "foyer_breshz1": "horizon$=$1, no hw",
    29	    "foyer_nogate": "no gate, no resv.", "foyer_rg4": "running gate$=$4",
    30	    "foyfix_hc": "all throttles off",
    31	    "aimd2": "AIMD, retuned", "aimd2_paper": "AIMD, paper params",
    32	}
    33	ENV_KEYS = ["cap4_hc", "cap4_hc_r2", "cap3_hc", "cap3_hc_r2", "pairB_cap2hc", "cap1"]  # 候选锚点（由上左包络自动筛）
    34	
    35	
    36	def load(p):
    37	    return list(csv.DictReader(l for l in open(p) if not l.startswith("#")))
    38	
    39	
    40	def _envelope(keys, runs):
    41	    """候选锚点的上左包络：按墙钟升序扫，SLO 破纪录的才留在前沿上。
    42	
    43	    本函数仍用来决定【谁在前沿上】（表里标 anchor 用），但 Δ 的插值不自己算 ——
    44	    见 main() 里对 frontier.py 的调用。
    45	    """
    46	    out, best = [], -1
    47	    for k in sorted(keys, key=lambda k: float(runs[k]["wall_min"])):
    48	        s = float(runs[k]["slo_pct"])
    49	        if s > best:
    50	            out.append((float(runs[k]["wall_min"]), s, k)); best = s
    51	    return out
    52	
    53	
    54	def _frontier_mod():
    55	    """把 frontier.py 作为唯一的前沿定义与插值来源 import 进来。
    56	
    57	    为什么必须共用：frontier.py 的 FRONT 锚点写死到 1 位小数（230.3 / 81.9），
    58	    而本脚本原来从 runs.csv 自己推包络再自己插值。两条各算各的，同一条臂
    59	    两边能差 0.02pp —— 表 I 写 -0.55、正文写 -0.57 就是这么来的
    60	    （2026-09-24 被审稿人抓到）。§repro 同时点名两个脚本，它们必须给同一个数。
    61	    """
    62	    import importlib.util
    63	    fp = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)), "frontier.py")
    64	    spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("_frontier", fp)
    65	    m = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
    66	    spec.loader.exec_module(m)
    67	    return m
    68	
    69	
    70	def _csv_str(x):
    71	    return x if isinstance(x, str) else str(x)
    72	
    73	
    74	def emit_csv(runs, env, delta, front_slo, conc, out_p):
    75	    """把表 I 的每一列落成一个 CSV —— 包括 Δ。
    76	
    77	    为什么必须落盘：论文声称「每个判决都由一个脚本产出」，而 Δ 是【渲染时算的】，
    78	    不存任何数据文件。审计于是把正文里每一个 Δ 数字都报成"找不到源头"
    79	    （2026-09-24 一类假阳性有十几个）。判决列必须和其它列一样可被引用。
    80	    """
    81	    FR = _frontier_mod()
    82	    fields = ["run", "label", "wall_min", "conc", "slo_pct", "front_slo",
    83	              "delta_pp", "verdict", "contested", "anchor"]
    84	    out = ["# 表 I 的每一列（含 Δ）—— 由 scripts/lab/gen_table.py 生成，勿手改",
    85	           "# front_slo = frontier.py 的 front_slo(wall)（同一函数，不是另写一遍）",
    86	           "# delta_pp  = slo_pct - front_slo；超出锚点范围为空",
    87	           "# verdict   = 按 ±1.0pp 判带：>+1 越过 / 在带内平局 / <-1 在线下",
    88	           "# contested = 同机争用窗口标记（concurrency.csv），与表里的 ‡ 同源",
    89	           ",".join(fields)]
    90	    env_w = {n for _, _, n in _envelope(ENV_KEYS, runs)}
    91	    lines = []
    92	    for k in sorted(runs, key=lambda k: float(runs[k]["wall_min"])):
    93	        r = runs[k]
    94	        w, sl = float(r["wall_min"]), float(r["slo_pct"])
    95	        f = FR.front_slo(w)
    96	        d = "" if f is None else "%.4f" % (sl - f)
    97	        v = ("OUT-OF-RANGE" if f is None else
    98	             "ABOVE" if sl - f > 1.0 else "TIE" if sl - f >= -1.0 else "BELOW")
    99	        # ⚠ 用 csv.writer 而不是手拼逗号：label 里有逗号（"AIMD, retuned"、
   100	        # "no gate, no resv." 等四条），手拼会让字段数多于表头、DictReader 整体错位
   101	        # （2026-09-24 审稿人抓）。csv.writer 会自动加引号。
   102	        import io as _io
   103	        buf = _io.StringIO()
   104	        _w = csv.writer(buf)
   105	        _w.writerow([_csv_str(x) for x in [
   106	            r["run"], LABEL.get(k, k), "%.4f" % w, r["conc"], "%.4f" % sl,
   107	            "" if f is None else "%.4f" % f, d, v, conc.get(k, "?"),
   108	            "1" if k in env_w else "0"]])
   109	        lines.append(buf.getvalue().rstrip("\r\n"))
   110	    body = "\n".join(out + lines) + "\n"
   111	    assert isinstance(body, str)
   112	    with open(out_p, "w") as fh:
   113	        fh.write(body)
   114	    print("  判决表 -> %s（%d 行）" % (out_p, len(lines)))
   115	
   116	
   117	def main():
   118	    runs = {r["run"]: r for r in load(sys.argv[1]) if r["tier"] == "200"}
   119	
   120	    # 全精度覆盖：runs.csv 是手抄 run_summary.py 的 1 位小数表，而 frontier.py
   121	    # 从原始日志算全精度（263.340318 vs 263.3）。同一条插值公式喂不同精度会差
   122	    # 0.02pp —— 表 I 写 -0.55、正文写 -0.57 就是这么来的（2026-09-24 被审稿人抓到）。
   123	    # 有 runs_precise.csv 就以它为准，两条管线共用一个来源。
   124	    cand_p = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(sys.argv[1])), "runs_precise.csv")
   125	    n_ov = 0
   126	    if os.path.exists(cand_p):
   127	        for r in load(cand_p):
   128	            k = r["run"].replace("pois200_", "")
   129	            if k in runs:
   130	                runs[k]["wall_min"] = r["wall_min"]
   131	                runs[k]["slo_pct"] = r["slo_pct"]
   132	                n_ov += 1
   133	        assert n_ov > 15, "runs_precise.csv 只覆盖了 %d 行，路径或前缀不对" % n_ov
   134	    else:
   135	        print("%% 警告：没有 runs_precise.csv，用 1 位小数的 runs.csv（delta 会与 frontier.py 差 ~0.02pp）",
   136	              file=sys.stderr)
   137	    env = [(w, s) for w, s, _ in _envelope(ENV_KEYS, runs)]
   138	
   139	    # 同机争用标记：读 concurrency.csv，被标 CONTESTED 的臂在表里加 \ddagger，
   140	    # 提醒读者这一行的墙钟/SLO/Δ 是在同机 >=4 条车道下测的、与其余各行不可比，
   141	    # 已重测（见 §repro）。表是脚本生成的，标记也必须从这里出，不能手加。
   142	    conc = {}
   143	    cand = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(sys.argv[1])), "concurrency.csv")
   144	    if os.path.exists(cand):
   145	        for r in load(cand):
   146	            conc[r["run"].replace("pois200_", "")] = r["verdict"]
   147	        assert len(conc) > 50, "concurrency.csv 只读到 %d 行" % len(conc)
   148	    # 短名（表里的 key）-> concurrency.csv 的短名：一致，直接查
   149	    def dagger(k):
   150	        return "$^\\ddagger$" if conc.get(k) == "CONTESTED" else ""
   151	
   152	    FR = _frontier_mod()          # 唯一的前沿定义与插值（见 _frontier_mod 的注释）
   153	
   154	    def front_slo(w):
   155	        return FR.front_slo(w)
   156	
   157	    def delta(k):
   158	        r = runs[k]
   159	        f = front_slo(float(r["wall_min"]))
   160	        return None if f is None else float(r["slo_pct"]) - f
   161	
   162	    def row(k, anchor=False):
   163	        r = runs[k]
   164	        d = delta(k)
   165	        ds = "\\emph{anchor}" if anchor else ("$+$%.2f" % d if d > 0 else "$-$%.2f" % -d)
   166	        if anchor:
   167	            ds = "\\emph{anchor}"
   168	        # 显示回 1 位小数（表里就是这样印的），但 ds 是用全精度算出来的
   169	        return "    %-17s & %.1f & %s & %.1f & %s \\\\" % (
   170	            LABEL[k] + dagger(k), float(r["wall_min"]), r["conc"],
   171	            float(r["slo_pct"]), ds)
   172	
   173	    print("    \\multicolumn{5}{l}{\\emph{Static caps}}\\\\")
   174	    for k in ["default", "cap1", "cap2", "cap3_fixed", "cap4", "cap5"]:
   175	        print(row(k, anchor=(k == "cap1")))
   176	    print("    \\midrule")
   177	    print("    \\multicolumn{5}{l}{\\emph{Static caps + HiCache}}\\\\")
   178	    # 只把【真正落在上左包络上】的点标成 anchor —— 不能按 ENV_KEYS 一律标，
   179	    # 因为 cap4_hc 已经被它自己的重复支配了，它不在前沿上。
   180	    on_front = {n for _, _, n in _envelope(ENV_KEYS, runs)}
   181	    for k in ["pairB_cap2hc", "cap3_hc", "cap3_hc_r2", "cap4_hc", "cap4_hc_r2"]:
   182	        print(row(k, anchor=(k in on_front)))
   183	    print("    \\midrule")
   184	    print("    \\multicolumn{5}{l}{\\emph{\\sys{} controller variants}}\\\\")
   185	    foyer = sorted([k for k in LABEL if k.startswith("foyer") or k == "foyfix_hc"],
   186	                   key=lambda k: -delta(k))
   187	    for k in foyer:
   188	        print(row(k))
   189	    print("    \\midrule")
   190	    print("    \\multicolumn{5}{l}{\\emph{Independent adaptive controller (\\S\\ref{sec:results:aimd})}}\\\\")
   191	    for k in sorted(["aimd2", "aimd2_paper"], key=lambda k: -delta(k)):
   192	        print(row(k))
   193	    # 同时把每一列（含 Δ）落成 CSV
   194	    import sys as _sys
   195	    if "--csv" in _sys.argv:
   196	        i = _sys.argv.index("--csv")
   197	        emit_csv(runs, env, delta, front_slo, conc, _sys.argv[i + 1])
   198	
   199	
   200	if __name__ == "__main__":
   201	    main()

    85	tuned; we obtain it two independent ways, and reporting both is what shows
    86	they are measuring different things rather than confirming each other.
    87	
    88	\paragraph{Method 1 (derived).} $\tau_1 = W_{\text{busy}} \cdot \overline{R} /
    89	G$. Two choices in that expression are easy to get wrong and both matter. The
    90	time term must be the \emph{busy} component, not the wall clock---using the
    91	wall clock charges idle time to the steps. And $\overline{R}$ must be the mean
    92	running count \emph{during busy time}, not the mean over the whole run: the
    93	whole-run mean includes the idle samples, where the count is zero, so it equals
    94	$\overline{R}_{\text{busy}} \times$ (busy fraction). Using it makes $\tau_1$
    95	too low by exactly the busy fraction---36.3\% for \texttt{cap1}, whose idle
    96	share is 36.3\%. We report both columns in the data, and use the
    97	busy-conditional mean throughout; the whole-run mean is what an earlier
    98	revision of this paper used, and it made the two methods agree far better than
    99	they do.
   100	
   101	\paragraph{Method 2 (independent).} For each step record we compute the
   102	per-request decode interval,
   103	$(\text{total\_duration} - \text{first\_token}) / (\text{output\_len} - 1)$,
   104	and take the median over steps with $\text{output\_len} \ge 32$.
   105	
   220	
   221	\subsection{The tier, not the policy, decides whether reuse survives}
   222	
   223	The device-miss column hides the quantity that matters. Define
   224	\emph{reuse} as the prompt tokens served from anywhere in the memory
   225	hierarchy---device radix cache plus host tier---so that
   226	$\text{reuse} + \text{recomputed} = \text{prompt}$, with the prompt fixed at
   227	28.5\,M by the trace.
   228	
   229	The bound on reuse has to be defined independently of what we observed,
   230	or the argument is circular. We take it from the trace: summing the planned
   231	reusable prefix (\texttt{prefix\_len}) over all 999 rounds of
   232	\texttt{replay\_pois200\_l04.csv} gives 21{,}540{,}371 tokens against a prompt
   233	total of 28{,}526{,}871, so no policy can reuse more than 75.5\% of the
   234	prompt. That is a property of the workload. It is also a bound rather than a
   235	description: \emph{no configuration we ran reaches it}, and the best realizes
   236	92.6\%, so the gap between ``planned'' and ``achieved'' exists even in the
   237	easiest case. What varies across configurations is how much of the bound is
   238	realized, and that is where the tier enters.
   239	
   240	\begin{center}
   241	\begin{tabular}{lcccccc}
   242	\toprule
   243	device-only & $\overline{R}_{\text{busy}}$ & 1.00 & 1.27 & 1.53 & 1.65 & 1.84 \\
   244	            & realized (\% of bound) & 92.6 & 89.7 & 79.0 & 60.7 & 36.4 \\
   245	            & recomputed (M)         &  8.6 &  9.2 & 11.5 & 15.4 & 20.7 \\
   246	\midrule
   247	host tier   & $\overline{R}_{\text{busy}}$ & 1.25 & 1.51 & 1.73 & & \\
   248	            & realized (\% of bound) & 92.4 & 91.3 & 90.2 & & \\
   249	            & recomputed (M)         &  8.6 &  8.9 &  9.1 & & \\
   250	\bottomrule
   251	\end{tabular}
   252	\end{center}
   253	
   254	Device-only, realization falls from 92.6\% to 36.4\% and the shortfall is
   255	recomputation: 8.6\,M to 20.7\,M tokens. With a host tier it holds at
   256	90.2--92.4\% on the static points that span the same range, and at
   257	84.2--92.4\% across the ten host-tier runs including the controller arms---the
   258	lowest of those being the arm that disables its own admission gate and
   259	therefore runs the pool hardest. and it holds
   260	while the \emph{device} misses more than the device-only family's at every
   261	point (22.3--27.9\,M against 8.6--20.7\,M), because the host serves
   262	13--19\,M in front of the recomputation instead of letting the device keep
   263	it. A reader watching only the device hit rate would conclude the host tier
   264	made things worse. (Two denominators appear here and we name them: the 75.5\%
   265	is the trace's bound as a share of the prompt, and 92.6\% is the best observed
   266	reuse as a share of \emph{that bound}---the same run reuses 69.9\% of its
   267	prompt. \texttt{reuse.csv} carries \texttt{reuse\_frac} and
   268	\texttt{realized\_frac} side by side so the two cannot be confused.)
   269	
   270	This is the same collapse \S\ref{sec:related} reports under a different
   271	normalization. That section quotes the round-1-and-later prefix hit rate---%
   272	steps restricted to rounds $\ge 1$, unweighted mean over steps---which falls
   273	from 80.8\% to 32.8\% across the device-only caps and holds at 80.7\% to
   274	78.7\% with a host tier. The two conventions differ in two ways, not one:
   275	which steps are in the set (round $\ge 1$ against all rounds) and how steps
   276	are combined (unweighted mean against token-weighted sum). We use the
   277	token-weighted all-steps convention here because reuse and recomputation must
   278	add to the prompt, and we name the other whenever we quote it. Both are
   279	\emph{realized} quantities that vary with the policy; neither is the trace's
   280	planned rate, which is fixed at 75.5\% and appears in this section only as
   281	the bound.
   282	
   283	Device occupancy does not explain the split. \texttt{cap3\_fixed} and
   284	\texttt{cap3\_hc} run at nearly the same mean device occupancy (55.8\% and
   285	55.5\%) and nearly the same running count (1.53 and 1.51), and they realize
   286	79.0\% and 91.3\% of the bound respectively. What differs is not how full the
   287	device pool is but what happens when it overflows: with no host tier an
   288	evicted prefix is gone and must be recomputed; with one it is demoted and read
   289	back. The pool is the binding resource, the tier sets the pool, and the tier
   290	therefore sets whether added concurrency is paid for in recomputation. The
   291	caveat is that this pair is two runs on two different days and two different
   292	GPUs, not a paired experiment; \S\ref{sec:repro} states what we did to rule
   293	out co-tenancy. A capacity-controlled version---same card, same trace, only
   294	the pool size changed---is queued behind the re-runs and we report it in
   295	\S\ref{sec:results} when it lands; we do not use it here.
     1	\section{Results: Four Routes, One Outcome}
     2	\label{sec:results}
     3	
     4	\subsection{The frontier}
     5	
     6	Fig.~\ref{fig:frontier} places every completed run on the (wall clock, SLO)
     7	plane and draws the upper-left envelope of the static points. Table~\ref{tab:runs}
     8	gives the numbers and each controller's signed distance from that envelope.
     9	
    10	The result is unambiguous. Of nine controller variants, \emph{none} is above
    11	the envelope---not one, on any run, and the pair we repeated most tightly says
    12	so at a resolution the others do not. The closest arm of all is the one with
    13	single-flight admission disabled, at $0.55$ percentage points \emph{below} the
    14	line---inside our $\pm1.0$\,pp tie band, so we record it as \emph{on} the
    15	line rather than below it, but on the below side of it.
    16	
    17	The base configuration is the one whose repeats we can trust, and they are
    18	tight: four runs spanning 0.5\,pp of SLO (89.5\% mean, 279.8 to 293.2 minutes),
    19	against the 3.3\,pp that separates it from the single-flight-off arm. So that
    20	difference is several times the noise of its own configuration rather than
    21	inside it, and it is a trade rather than a win: 28 minutes of wall clock bought
    22	with 3.5\,pp of SLO, moving the arm onto the tie band from below it. Not one
    23	configuration in the table gets above the line, and the tightest pair we have
    24	is what makes us willing to say the near misses are misses. A fifth pair, two runs of \texttt{cap3}+HiCache, \emph{is} used, and an
    25	earlier draft of this paper wrongly set it aside saying one of them exceeded
    26	its own cap. That was a different run: the over-cap one is the device-only
    27	\texttt{cap3} repeat (peak in-flight five against a cap of three; at our
    28	300-point in-flight sample grid, 126 samples sit over the cap, a count that
    29	scales with the grid rather than with the run), while both HiCache runs peak at three with none. The
    30	repeat is also both faster and higher on SLO (230.3 minutes, 81.9\% against
    31	236.4 and 81.1\%), so by the same rule that displaced \texttt{cap4}+HiCache's
    32	first run it displaces this one---and moving that anchor is what puts our
    33	best arm below the line rather than on it.\footnote{The repeats are uneven, and
    34	we do not want the tightest pair to stand for all of them. Five configurations
    35	have been run more than once; their SLO spreads are 0.5, 0.8, 1.0, 3.9 and
   205	be \emph{candidates that did not fit}, since the gate cannot be holding---falls
   206	from 54.8\% of blocked cycles to 39.7\%, against 44.0\% that are over budget
   207	outright. So the single-flight path accounts for roughly fifteen points, and
   208	the headroom estimate remains the largest single cause. (Both arms' numbers
   209	are rows of \texttt{gate\_attrib.csv}, which now includes the \texttt{sf=0}
   210	arm; an earlier revision quoted 39.9 and 43.5 for the second pair, values that
   211	appeared in no data file.)
   212	
   213	The second thing is that disabling it does not make the controller win.
   214	\texttt{sf=0} finishes in 263.3 minutes at 86.3\% SLO against the base run's
   215	291.3 and 89.8\%: it buys 28 minutes for 3.5\,pp, and its signed distance from
   216	the front moves from $-1.24$ to $-0.55$---onto the tie band from below it, not
   217	above it. Its admission rate is unchanged (2.5\% of cycles admit, against
   218	2.3\%). So the gate was a real cost and removing it moves the arm along the
   219	front rather than past it, which is the same trade every other route in this
   220	section makes.
   221	
   222	Across the eight \sys{} variants that left controller logs---the set is named in
   223	\texttt{gate\_attrib.csv}, and it is eight rather than nine because
   224	\texttt{foyfix} and the two AIMD arms run a different controller and log no
   225	gate state---the projection is over budget in a median 32.2\% of blocked
    25	trace's 15.8\,s; we use the transition definition throughout §\ref{sec:xtrace}. So the engine is idle far more of the
    26	time, and the running counts are lower.
    27	
    28	\subsection{What transfers, and what does not}
    29	
    30	\paragraph{The reuse accounting transfers in direction, not in level.} The
    31	second trace plans a higher reuse bound than the first---86.3\% of its prompt
    32	tokens against 75.5\%---and realizes less of it, at a lower device occupancy.
    33	Realization falls from 74.8\% to 64.0\% as the running count rises from 1.08 to
    34	1.41, so the direction of \S\ref{sec:model} survives. What does \emph{not}
    35	survive is the shape. On the main trace the decline is a threshold: the
    36	per-unit slope is $-10.4$, $-40.6$, then $-157.8$ and $-127.0$ points per unit
    37	as the running count passes 1.53, which is the cliff \S\ref{sec:criterion}
    38	measures. Here the slope is roughly constant---$-31.2$, $-36.8$, $-28.4$---over
    39	a span that reaches 1.41, so at 1.41 this trace has lost more of its bound
    40	than the main trace had at 1.53 (36\% against 21\%) and has not yet begun to
    41	fall off a cliff. The cliff is a threshold that this trace has not reached at
    42	the running counts its own offered load produces; whether it has one at all,
    43	and where, is what the rest of the sweep is for. We do compare the two
    44	traces' overall slopes, which is the one slope comparison the data supports
    45	because both are taken over their full measured ranges
    46	(\texttt{slope.csv}): the device-only family runs from 92.5\% to 36.4\% over
    47	$\overline{R}$ 1.000--1.842, a whole-range slope of $-66.7$ points per unit,
    48	and this trace from 74.8\% to 64.0\% over 1.076--1.409, a slope of $-32.3$.
    49	So per unit of running count, the main trace loses its bound twice as fast
    50	over its range---but the comparison is between ranges of different length and
    51	different absolute level, and we do not lean on it. The claim we do lean on is
    52	the shape one below. The level does not, and we treat that as a bound on what we can
    53	claim: this trace realizes \emph{less} at 15.8\% median device occupancy than the
    54	main trace does at 22.4\% (both medians of \texttt{sglang:token\_usage}; the
    55	means are 13.9\% and 25.2\%), so realization is not a function of occupancy alone and a
    56	rule of the form ``the pool collapses above $x$\% occupancy'' is false on our
    57	own data. Occupancy is instantaneous; reuse depends on whether a prefix
    58	survives from one round to the next, which is a history the instantaneous
    59	number does not carry. We do not measure that history here, and we say so
    60	rather than fitting the two traces to a common curve.

 if len(v)>=2:print(k,len(v),' '.join(v[:25]))

     1	#!/usr/bin/env python3
     2	"""从原始日志重新生成 paper/data/decomp.csv —— 论文 C1/C2/C3 的唯一数据来源。
     3	
     4	为什么要有这个脚本：这张表原来是在终端里手算然后粘进 CSV 的，结果
     5	`pairB_cap2hc` 那一行的 busy/idle 被写成了另一条 run 的值（总长 220.9 分），
     6	而 wall 填的是正确的 295.0 —— 两个数自相矛盾，图 2 因此画出一根短得离谱的
     7	柱子，标签飘在右边 80 分钟处。手算的表没有残差检查，所以这种错误不会被发现。
     8	
     9	本脚本对每条 run 做三件事，并且**强制**检查：
    10	  1. busy + idle 必须精确等于采样跨度 span（残差 != 0 直接报错退出）
    11	  2. span 与 run_summary 的 wall 之差必须落在 [0, 12] 分钟（采样边界造成的固定偏移）
    12	  3. 任何一步失败就把该 run 标成 INVALID，不写入 CSV
    13	
    14	用法:  python3 gen_decomp.py <results_dir> <out.csv> [run ...]
    15	"""
    16	import csv, json, os, statistics, sys, glob
    17	
    18	HEADER = ["run", "wall_min", "span_min", "idle_min", "busy_min", "idle_pct",
    19	          "running", "running_all", "step_back_ms", "step_perreq_ms",
    20	          # τ₁/τ₂ 的超出比例 —— §III 引用「τ₁ 比 τ₂ 高 7.9~39.8%」这个区间。
    21	          # 它是两个导出量之比，此前不落盘（审计报成"找不到源头"）。
    22	          "excess_pct"]
    23	
    24	# 采样周期。metrics.csv 每 5 秒一行。
    25	SAMPLE_S = 5.0
    26	# 采样跨度允许比 run_summary 的墙钟长多少（秒）。采样器比第一个请求早启动、
    27	# 比最后一个请求晚停下，这个偏移在全部 run 上是恒定的 ~6 分钟。
    28	SPAN_SLACK_S = (0.0, 12 * 60)
    29	
    30	
    31	def read_metrics(path):
    32	    """返回 [(ts, num_running_reqs_or_None)]。"""
    33	    rows = []
    34	    with open(path) as f:
    35	        hdr = f.readline().strip().split(",")
    36	        if "sglang:num_running_reqs" not in hdr:
    37	            raise RuntimeError("%s 没有 sglang:num_running_reqs 列" % path)
    38	        i_ts = hdr.index("ts")
    39	        i_rn = hdr.index("sglang:num_running_reqs")
    40	        for line in f:
    41	            c = line.rstrip("\n").split(",")
    42	            if len(c) <= max(i_ts, i_rn):
    43	                continue
    44	            try:
    45	                ts = float(c[i_ts])
    46	            except ValueError:
    47	                continue
    48	            try:
    49	                rn = float(c[i_rn])
    50	            except ValueError:
    51	                rn = None
    52	            rows.append((ts, rn))
    53	    if len(rows) < 10:
    54	        raise RuntimeError("%s 只有 %d 个采样点" % (path, len(rows)))
    55	    return rows
    56	
    57	
    58	def decompose(rows):
    59	    """把相邻样本之间的间隔按【前一个】样本的状态归类。
    60	
    61	    这是论文 C1 的定义。返回分钟数；残差必须为 0。
    62	    """
    63	    span = rows[-1][0] - rows[0][0]
    64	    busy = idle = other = 0.0
    65	    for (t0, r0), (t1, _) in zip(rows, rows[1:]):
    66	        dt = t1 - t0
    67	        if dt <= 0 or dt > SAMPLE_S * 3:      # 采样断档：既不算 busy 也不算 idle
    68	            other += dt
    69	            continue
    70	        if r0 is None:
    71	            other += dt
    72	        elif r0 == 0:
    73	            idle += dt
    74	        else:
    75	            busy += dt
    76	    resid = span - busy - idle - other
    77	    assert abs(resid) < 1e-6, "分解残差 %.6f s != 0" % resid
    78	    assert other == 0.0, "有 %.1f s 采样断档，该 run 的分解不可信" % other
    79	    return span / 60, busy / 60, idle / 60, 100 * idle / span
    80	
    81	
    82	def read_steps(path):
    83	    out = []
    84	    with open(path) as f:
    85	        for line in f:
    86	            line = line.strip()
    87	            if line:
    88	                try:
    89	                    out.append(json.loads(line))
    90	                except json.JSONDecodeError:
    91	                    pass
    92	    return out
    93	
    94	
    95	def step_perreq_ms(steps):
    96	    """方法二：逐请求 decode 间隔的中位。只取 output_len >= 32 的步。"""
    97	    vals = []
    98	    for s in steps:
    99	        n = s.get("output_len_actual") or 0
   100	        tot = s.get("total_duration_ms")
   101	        ft = s.get("first_token_ms")
   102	        if n >= 32 and tot is not None and ft is not None and n > 1:
   103	            vals.append((tot - ft) / (n - 1))
   104	    return statistics.median(vals) if vals else None
   105	
   106	
   107	def main():
   108	    root = sys.argv[1]
   109	    out_path = sys.argv[2]
   110	    runs = sys.argv[3:]
   111	    if not runs:
   112	        runs = sorted(os.path.basename(os.path.dirname(p))
   113	                      for p in glob.glob(os.path.join(root, "*", "metrics.csv")))
   114	
   115	    rows, bad = [], []
   116	    for r in runs:
   117	        d = os.path.join(root, r)
   118	        try:
   119	            rows_m = read_metrics(os.path.join(d, "metrics.csv"))
   120	            span_min, busy_min, idle_min, idle_pct = decompose(rows_m)
   121	            steps = read_steps(os.path.join(d, "steps.jsonl"))
   122	            tokens = sum(s.get("output_len_actual") or 0 for s in steps)
   123	
   124	            # 墙钟的口径必须和 run_summary.py 逐字一致，否则图 2 的柱子长度
   125	            # 和图 1 的横坐标就是两个数。见 run_summary.py:91：
   126	            #   墙钟 = 最后一个完成 − 最早一个提交
   127	            # 不能用 st[0]：steps.jsonl 是按完成时间排的。
   128	            subs = [s["submit_timestamp"] for s in steps if s.get("submit_timestamp")]
   129	            comps = [s["complete_timestamp"] for s in steps if s.get("complete_timestamp")]
   130	            if not subs or not comps:
   131	                bad.append((r, "steps.jsonl 缺 submit/complete 时间戳"))
   132	                continue
   133	            wall_min = (max(comps) - min(subs)) / 60.0
   134	
   135	            if not (SPAN_SLACK_S[0] <= (span_min - wall_min) * 60 <= SPAN_SLACK_S[1]):
   136	                bad.append((r, "采样跨度 %.1f 分与墙钟 %.1f 分差 %.1f 分，超出容许的 0-12 分"
   137	                            % (span_min, wall_min, span_min - wall_min)))
   138	                continue
   139	
   140	            running_all = statistics.mean(v for _, v in rows_m if v is not None)
   141	            # τ 的分母必须是【busy 期间的条件均值】，不是全时段均值。
   142	            #
   143	            # 式子 W_busy ≈ G/R̄ · τ 里，R̄ 是「每一步在跑几个请求」；而全时段
   144	            # 采样均值把空转的那些 0 也算进去了，等于 R̄_busy × busy 占比。
   145	            # 拿它乘 busy 时间，得到的 τ 会系统性地【低】一个 busy 占比：
   146	            # cap1 空转 36.3%，于是 18.5ms 而不是真实的 ~28.9ms。
   147	            # 这个错误曾经让「两个测量相差 12% 以内」这句话成立 —— 其实是
   148	            # 两个错误互相抵消。
   149	            busy_frac = busy_min / span_min if span_min else 1.0
   150	            running = running_all / busy_frac if busy_frac > 0 else running_all
   151	            step_back = (busy_min * 60 * running / tokens * 1000) if tokens else None
   152	            rows.append({
   153	                "run": r.replace("pois200_", ""),
   154	                "wall_min": round(wall_min, 1),
   155	                "span_min": round(span_min, 1),
   156	                "idle_min": round(idle_min, 1),
   157	                "busy_min": round(busy_min, 1),
   158	                "idle_pct": round(idle_pct, 1),
   159	                "running": round(running, 2), "running_all": round(running_all, 2),
   160	                "step_back_ms": round(step_back, 1) if step_back else "",
   161	                "step_perreq_ms": (round(step_perreq_ms(steps), 1)
   162	                                   if step_perreq_ms(steps) else ""),
   163	                # τ₁/τ₂ 超出比例。两个量都非空才算，否则留空。
   164	                "excess_pct": (round(100 * (step_back / step_perreq_ms(steps) - 1), 1)
   165	                               if (step_back and step_perreq_ms(steps)) else ""),
   166	            })
   167	        except Exception as e:
   168	            bad.append((r, str(e)))
   169	
   170	    if bad:
   171	        print("!! 以下 run 未通过校验，未写入 CSV：")
   172	        for r, why in bad:
   173	            print("   %-24s %s" % (r, why))
   174	
   175	    with open(out_path, "w", newline="") as f:
   176	        f.write("# 墙钟的两段分解 + 步时间 —— 由 scripts/lab/gen_decomp.py 生成，勿手改\n")
   177	        f.write("# busy/idle: 相邻采样按前一时刻 sglang:num_running_reqs 是否为 0 归类；busy+idle==span（有断言）\n")
   178	        f.write("# span_min: metrics.csv 的采样跨度；wall_min: run_summary 的墙钟。两者差 ~6 分，是采样边界\n")
   179	        f.write("# step_back: busy分钟 x 在跑均值 / 产出token（导出量；用 busy 不用墙钟，见下）; step_perreq: 逐请求 decode 间隔中位（独立测量）\n")
   180	        w = csv.DictWriter(f, fieldnames=HEADER)
   181	        w.writeheader()
   182	        w.writerows(rows)
   183	    print("写入 %s：%d 行" % (out_path, len(rows)))
   184	
   185	
   186	if __name__ == "__main__":
   187	    main()
     1	#!/usr/bin/env python3
     2	"""空转段的合并统计（全部 vs 排除受争用影响的 run）—— paper/data/idle_pooled.csv。
     3	
     4	为什么要有：论文 §III 引用「1849 段，中位 5.6 s，均值 20.6 s，idle 时间中 69.6%
     5	在 >20 s 的段里」。但那 1849 段里含四条被同机争用影响的 run（争用抬高 τ，
     6	busy 变长、idle 变短，所以段时长【可能】被影响）。本脚本回答：排除它们之后
     7	这些数会变吗？
     8	
     9	答案是不会（中位 5.60→5.00 s，均值 20.65→21.02 s，>20 s 占比 69.6%→70.6%）。
    10	这是因为段时长由【工具等待】决定，是工作负载属性，不是步成本的函数 ——
    11	所以这个检查反过来支持了论文「空转是结构性的」这一主张。
    12	
    13	用法: python3 gen_idle_pooled.py paper/data/idle_seg_raw.csv paper/data/concurrency.csv <out.csv>
    14	"""
    15	import csv, statistics, sys
    16	
    17	BAD = None
    18	
    19	
    20	def load(p):
    21	    return [r for r in csv.DictReader(l for l in open(p) if not l.startswith("#"))]
    22	
    23	
    24	def main():
    25	    raw_p, conc_p, out_p = sys.argv[1:4]
    26	    bad = {r["run"].replace("pois200_", "") for r in load(conc_p)
    27	           if r["verdict"] == "CONTESTED"}
    28	    rows = load(raw_p)
    29	    assert len(rows) > 1000, "原始段只有 %d 条，路径或格式不对" % len(rows)
    30	
    31	    # 四个变体都出：合并在位/均值对【并哪些 run】的敏感性本身就是要报的东西。
    32	    # 实测：中位被 cap1 一条主导（它占 22% 的段，自身中位 5.0 s），去掉它中位
    33	    # 从 5.60 跳到 10.00；而均值（18.3~21.0）与 >20s 占比（64.8~70.6%）稳定。
    34	    damaged = {"foyer_hz2", "foyer_hz0", "foyer_bres"}   # 实测被争用损害的三条
    35	    out = []
    36	    for name, excl in (("all_runs", set()),
    37	                       ("minus_cap1", {"cap1"}),
    38	                       ("minus_damaged3", damaged),
    39	                       ("minus_contested4", bad & {r["run"] for r in rows})):
    40	        sel = [r for r in rows if r["run"] not in excl]
    41	        v = [float(r["duration_s"]) for r in sel]
    42	        tot = sum(v)
    43	        out.append({
    44	            "set": name, "n_segments": len(v), "n_runs": len({r["run"] for r in sel}),
    45	            "median_s": "%.2f" % statistics.median(v),
    46	            "mean_s": "%.2f" % statistics.mean(v),
    47	            "p90_s": "%.2f" % sorted(v)[int(0.9 * len(v))],
    48	            "max_s": "%.1f" % max(v),
    49	            "pct_idle_time_in_gt20s": "%.1f" % (100 * sum(x for x in v if x > 20) / tot),
    50	            "pct_segments_gt20s": "%.1f" % (100 * sum(1 for x in v if x > 20) / len(v)),
    51	        })
    52	
    53	    header = "\n".join([
    54	        "# 空转段的合并统计 —— 由 scripts/lab/gen_idle_pooled.py 生成，勿手改",
    55	        "# clean_only = 排除 concurrency.csv 里判 CONTESTED 的 run（它们的 τ 被同机争用抬高）",
    56	        "# 四个变体：中位与 p90 被 cap1 主导（它占 22% 的段），均值与 >20s 占比稳定。",
    57	        "# 论文引用的「中位 5.6 s」必须写成区间；「均值 ~20 s」与「>20s 占 idle 时间 ~2/3」稳健。",
    58	        "set,n_segments,n_runs,median_s,mean_s,p90_s,max_s,pct_idle_time_in_gt20s,pct_segments_gt20s"])
    59	    fields = ["set", "n_segments", "n_runs", "median_s", "mean_s", "p90_s", "max_s",
    60	              "pct_idle_time_in_gt20s", "pct_segments_gt20s"]
    61	    body = header + "\n" + "\n".join(",".join(str(r[k]) for k in fields) for r in out) + "\n"
    62	    assert isinstance(body, str)
    63	    with open(out_p, "w") as fh:
    64	        fh.write(body)
    65	    print("写入 %s" % out_p)
    66	    for r in out:
    67	        print("  %-11s n=%4d(%2d run) 中位 %5s 均值 %5s  p90 %6s  >20s 占 idle 时间 %5s%%"
    68	              % (r["set"], r["n_segments"], r["n_runs"], r["median_s"], r["mean_s"],
    69	                 r["p90_s"], r["pct_idle_time_in_gt20s"]))
    70	
    71	
    72	if __name__ == "__main__":
    73	    main()

     1	#!/usr/bin/env python3
     2	"""把 run 放到静态前沿上判「赢了没有」。
     3	
     4	用法:  python3 frontier.py <run> [<run> ...]
     5	       python3 frontier.py --all-foyer
     6	
     7	为什么需要它：我们反复在"这个数比那个数好"上打转，而正确的判据只有一个——
     8	**这个点在不在这条 (墙钟, SLO) 静态前沿的上方**。落在两个静态 cap 中间的连线上
     9	不算赢，因为静态方法按时间片混合就能逼近线上任一点（§二十七、§五十二）。
    10	
    11	前沿的三个锚点（全部 999 步、HiCache 开、同一代码代）：
    12	    cap4_hc_r2 (209.1, 68.5)   # 重复 run 支配了 cap4_hc，见 paper/data/runs.csv
    13	    cap3_hc_r2 (230.3, 81.9)   # 2026-09-24 更正：cap3_hc 的重复同样在两个轴上都支配它，
    14	                               # 按同一条规矩必须顶掉。原稿以"它超了自己的 cap"为由排除，
    15	                               # 但那是【另一条非 HiCache 的 pois200_cap3_r2】（峰值在飞 5、
    16	                               # 超帽 126 个样本）；cap3_hc_r2 峰值在飞 3、超帽 0，是干净的。
    17	    cap2_hc  (295.0, 91.6)   <- pairB；pairA (304.5, 90.6) 被它支配，不入前沿
    18	低于 213 分钟没有任何静态点，落在那里是平凡获胜。
    19	"""
    20	import json, os, statistics as st, sys
    21	
    22	RESULTS = "/data/xbw/turnstile/results/night"
    23	# 上左包络的锚点，按墙钟升序。cap1 没有 HiCache 版本，但它仍然是"静态 cap 能达到的点"，
    24	# 必须放进前沿——否则墙钟超过最右锚点的 run 会被误判成"越过前沿"（cap1 自己就撞过这个）。
    25	# 锚点坐标必须是【全精度】，不能用表里印的 1 位小数。
    26	# 2026-09-24 审稿人抓：这里原来写死 209.1/68.5 等显示值，于是【全篇的 Δ 都是用
    27	# 显示值插值出来的】—— sf0 得 -0.57（全精度 -0.55）、hz1 得 -0.94（-0.93）；
    28	# 更糟的是锚点自己的 Δ 不为 0（cap4_hc_r2 -0.055、cap3_hc_r2 -0.019），
    29	# 而 cap1 因为墙钟 527.4320 > 锚点 527.4 被判成 OUT-OF-RANGE。
    30	# 上一轮把 gen_table 改成 import 本模块，两条管线【一致】了 —— 但一致在显示值上。
    31	# 坐标来自 paper/data/runs_precise.csv（frontier.py --csv 的产物），逐位相符。
    32	FRONT = [(209.137154, 68.468468, "cap4_hc_r2"),
    33	         (230.308193, 81.881882, "cap3_hc_r2"),
    34	         (295.020816, 91.591592, "pairB_cap2hc"),
    35	         (527.431963, 94.794795, "cap1")]
    36	
    37	
    38	def front_slo(wall):
    39	    """前沿在给定墙钟处的 SLO 上界（分段线性插值）。超出锚点范围返回 None——
    40	    范围外没有可比的前沿，不能给判决，否则端点外推会凭空造出"越过"。"""
    41	    # 容差：锚点 run 自己的墙钟与 FRONT 里的字面量会差几个 ulp，不加容差会把
    42	    # 锚点自己判成 LEFT-OF-FRONT（2026-09-24 全精度化之后暴露出来的）。
    43	    EPS = 1e-6
    44	    if wall < FRONT[0][0] - EPS or wall > FRONT[-1][0] + EPS:
    45	        return None
    46	    for (w0, s0, _), (w1, s1, _) in zip(FRONT, FRONT[1:]):
    47	        # 容差也要加在这里：只加在上面的范围检查是不够的 —— 锚点 run 自己的墙钟
    48	        # 比 FRONT 里的字面量小 5.7e-8，于是 w0 <= wall 为假、整条循环都不匹配，
    49	        # 锚点被自己的前沿判成 LEFT-OF-FRONT（2026-09-24 全精度化之后暴露的）。
    50	        if w0 - EPS <= wall <= w1 + EPS:
    51	            return s0 + (s1 - s0) * (wall - w0) / (w1 - w0)
    52	    return None
    53	
    54	
    55	def summarise(run):
    56	    p = os.path.join(RESULTS, run, "steps.jsonl")
    57	    if not os.path.exists(p):
    58	        return None
    59	    rows = [json.loads(l) for l in open(p)]
    60	    ts = [r["submit_timestamp"] for r in rows if r.get("submit_timestamp")]
    61	    te = [r["complete_timestamp"] for r in rows if r.get("complete_timestamp")]
    62	    tt = [r["first_token_ms"] for r in rows if r.get("first_token_ms") is not None]
    63	    if not ts or not tt:
    64	        return None
    65	    wall = (max(te) - min(ts)) / 60.0
    66	    slo = 100.0 * sum(1 for x in tt if x <= 10000) / len(tt)
    67	    # 实测并发：按 30 秒采样活跃请求数
    68	    N = 300
    69	    act = []
    70	    for i in range(N):
    71	        t = min(ts) + (max(te) - min(ts)) * i / (N - 1)
    72	        act.append(sum(1 for r in rows if r.get("submit_timestamp") and r.get("complete_timestamp")
    73	                       and r["submit_timestamp"] <= t < r["complete_timestamp"]))
    74	    # 分段 SLO 标准差（十等分）
    75	    seg = []
    76	    for i in range(10):
    77	        s = tt[i * len(tt) // 10:(i + 1) * len(tt) // 10]
    78	        if s:
    79	            seg.append(100.0 * sum(1 for x in s if x <= 10000) / len(s))
    80	    sd = st.pstdev(seg) if len(seg) > 1 else 0.0
    81	    return dict(run=run, steps=len(rows), wall=wall, conc=sum(act) / N, slo=slo, sd=sd)
    82	
    83	
    84	def verdict(d):
    85	    f = front_slo(d["wall"])
    86	    d["front"] = float("nan") if f is None else f
    87	    if f is None:
    88	        d["delta"] = float("nan")
    89	        d["verdict"] = ("LEFT-OF-FRONT (无静态点在更左, 平凡获胜)"
    90	                        if d["wall"] < FRONT[0][0] else "OUT-OF-RANGE (超出现有静态点范围)")
    91	        return d
    92	    d["delta"] = d["slo"] - f
    93	    if d["delta"] > 1.0:
    94	        d["verdict"] = "ABOVE  (越过前沿)"
    95	    elif d["delta"] > -1.0:
    96	        d["verdict"] = "ON     (在线上, 不算赢)"
    97	    else:
    98	        d["verdict"] = "BELOW  (在线下)"
    99	    return d
   100	
   101	
   102	def emit_csv():
   103	    """全精度导出 —— 给 gen_table.py 用。
   104	
   105	    为什么需要：runs.csv 是手抄 run_summary.py 打印的【1 位小数】表得来的，
   106	    而本脚本从原始日志算的是全精度。同一条插值公式，输入 263.3/86.3 与
   107	    263.340318/86.286286 会给出差 0.02pp 的 delta —— 于是表 I 写 -0.55、
   108	    正文写 -0.57（2026-09-24 审稿人抓到的对不上）。让两条管线共用这一个
   109	    全精度来源，而不是各自去读不同精度的表。
   110	    """
   111	    import csv as _csv
   112	    w = _csv.writer(sys.stdout)
   113	    w.writerow(["run", "wall_min", "slo_pct"])
   114	    for nm in sorted(os.listdir(RESULTS)):
   115	        if not nm.startswith("pois200_"):
   116	            continue
   117	        d = summarise(nm)
   118	        if not d or d["steps"] < 999:
   119	            continue
   120	        w.writerow([nm, "%.6f" % d["wall"], "%.6f" % d["slo"]])
   121	
   122	
   123	def main():
   124	    runs = sys.argv[1:]
   125	    if runs == ["--csv"]:
   126	        emit_csv()
   127	        return 0
   128	    if runs == ["--all-foyer"]:
   129	        runs = sorted(r for r in os.listdir(RESULTS)
   130	                      if r.startswith("pois200_foyer") or r.startswith("pois200_cap"))
   131	    out = []
   132	    for r in runs:
   133	        d = summarise(r)
   134	        if d and d["steps"] >= 999:
   135	            out.append(verdict(d))
   136	    print("%-30s %5s %8s %7s %7s %8s %8s  %s" %
   137	          ("run", "步数", "墙钟min", "并发", "SLO%", "前沿SLO", "差(pp)", "判决"))
   138	    for d in sorted(out, key=lambda x: x["wall"]):
   139	        print("%-30s %5d %8.1f %7.2f %7.1f %8.1f %+8.2f  %s" %
   140	              (d["run"], d["steps"], d["wall"], d["conc"], d["slo"], d["front"], d["delta"], d["verdict"]))
   141	    print("\n注意：步数 < 999 的 run 一律不列（未跑完的不能与跑完的比，§多处的教训）。")
   142	
   143	
   144	if __name__ == "__main__":
   145	    main()
     1	#!/usr/bin/env python3
     2	"""从 runs.csv 生成论文表 I 的 LaTeX 表体 —— 让「每个判决都由一个脚本产出」成为真的。
     3	
     4	为什么必须有：表 I 的 Δ 列原来是手抄的，其中两行抄错了（rg4 写成 −24.0 实际
     5	−25.02；foyfix 写成 −48.7 实际 −47.15）。而正文第 §7 声称每个判决都由单一脚本
     6	从原始日志产出 —— 手抄的表让这句话不成立。这个脚本把表变成可复现的。
     7	
     8	判决口径与 scripts/lab/frontier.py 一致：
     9	  包络 = 静态点（含 +HC）的上左包络
    10	  Δ = 该点的 SLO − 包络在同墙钟处的插值 SLO
    11	  判读：Δ > +1.0 越过 / −1.0 ≤ Δ ≤ +1.0 平局 / Δ < −1.0 在线下
    12	
    13	用法: python3 gen_table.py ../paper/data/runs.csv
    14	"""
    15	import csv, os, sys
    16	
    17	# 表里的行标签（与论文一致），以及它们的排序位置由 Δ 决定
    18	LABEL = {
    19	    "default": "no control",
    20	    "cap1": "cap1", "cap2": "cap2", "cap3_fixed": "cap3 (fixed)",
    21	    "cap4": "cap4", "cap5": "cap5",
    22	    "pairB_cap2hc": "cap2+HC", "cap3_hc": "cap3+HC", "cap4_hc": "cap4+HC",
    23	    "cap4_hc_r2": "cap4+HC (repeat)", "cap3_hc_r2": "cap3+HC (repeat)",
    24	    "foyer_hc_r2": "horizon$=$3 (2nd run)", "foyer_sf0_r2": "single-flight off",
    25	    "foyer_hz1": "horizon$=$1",
    26	    "foyer_hz1_r2": "horizon$=$1 (repeat)", "foyer_hc": "horizon$=$3 (base)",
    27	    "foyer_hz2": "horizon$=$2", "foyer_bres": "resident base",
    28	    "foyer_hz0": "horizon$=$0", "foyer_breshz1": "horizon$=$1, no hw",
    29	    "foyer_nogate": "no gate, no resv.", "foyer_rg4": "running gate$=$4",
    30	    "foyfix_hc": "all throttles off",
    31	    "aimd2": "AIMD, retuned", "aimd2_paper": "AIMD, paper params",
    32	}
    33	ENV_KEYS = ["cap4_hc", "cap4_hc_r2", "cap3_hc", "cap3_hc_r2", "pairB_cap2hc", "cap1"]  # 候选锚点（由上左包络自动筛）
    34	
    35	
    36	def load(p):
    37	    return list(csv.DictReader(l for l in open(p) if not l.startswith("#")))
    38	
    39	
    40	def _envelope(keys, runs):
    41	    """候选锚点的上左包络：按墙钟升序扫，SLO 破纪录的才留在前沿上。
    42	
    43	    本函数仍用来决定【谁在前沿上】（表里标 anchor 用），但 Δ 的插值不自己算 ——
    44	    见 main() 里对 frontier.py 的调用。
    45	    """
    46	    out, best = [], -1
    47	    for k in sorted(keys, key=lambda k: float(runs[k]["wall_min"])):
    48	        s = float(runs[k]["slo_pct"])
    49	        if s > best:
    50	            out.append((float(runs[k]["wall_min"]), s, k)); best = s
    51	    return out
    52	
    53	
    54	def _frontier_mod():
    55	    """把 frontier.py 作为唯一的前沿定义与插值来源 import 进来。
    56	
    57	    为什么必须共用：frontier.py 的 FRONT 锚点写死到 1 位小数（230.3 / 81.9），
    58	    而本脚本原来从 runs.csv 自己推包络再自己插值。两条各算各的，同一条臂
    59	    两边能差 0.02pp —— 表 I 写 -0.55、正文写 -0.57 就是这么来的
    60	    （2026-09-24 被审稿人抓到）。§repro 同时点名两个脚本，它们必须给同一个数。
    61	    """
    62	    import importlib.util
    63	    fp = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)), "frontier.py")
    64	    spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("_frontier", fp)
    65	    m = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
    66	    spec.loader.exec_module(m)
    67	    return m
    68	
    69	
    70	def _csv_str(x):
    71	    return x if isinstance(x, str) else str(x)
    72	
    73	
    74	def emit_csv(runs, env, delta, front_slo, conc, out_p):
    75	    """把表 I 的每一列落成一个 CSV —— 包括 Δ。
    76	
    77	    为什么必须落盘：论文声称「每个判决都由一个脚本产出」，而 Δ 是【渲染时算的】，
    78	    不存任何数据文件。审计于是把正文里每一个 Δ 数字都报成"找不到源头"
    79	    （2026-09-24 一类假阳性有十几个）。判决列必须和其它列一样可被引用。
    80	    """
    81	    FR = _frontier_mod()
    82	    fields = ["run", "label", "wall_min", "conc", "slo_pct", "front_slo",
    83	              "delta_pp", "verdict", "contested", "anchor"]
    84	    out = ["# 表 I 的每一列（含 Δ）—— 由 scripts/lab/gen_table.py 生成，勿手改",
    85	           "# front_slo = frontier.py 的 front_slo(wall)（同一函数，不是另写一遍）",
    86	           "# delta_pp  = slo_pct - front_slo；超出锚点范围为空",
    87	           "# verdict   = 按 ±1.0pp 判带：>+1 越过 / 在带内平局 / <-1 在线下",
    88	           "# contested = 同机争用窗口标记（concurrency.csv），与表里的 ‡ 同源",
    89	           ",".join(fields)]
    90	    env_w = {n for _, _, n in _envelope(ENV_KEYS, runs)}
    91	    lines = []
    92	    for k in sorted(runs, key=lambda k: float(runs[k]["wall_min"])):
    93	        r = runs[k]
    94	        w, sl = float(r["wall_min"]), float(r["slo_pct"])
    95	        f = FR.front_slo(w)
    96	        d = "" if f is None else "%.4f" % (sl - f)
    97	        v = ("OUT-OF-RANGE" if f is None else
    98	             "ABOVE" if sl - f > 1.0 else "TIE" if sl - f >= -1.0 else "BELOW")
    99	        # ⚠ 用 csv.writer 而不是手拼逗号：label 里有逗号（"AIMD, retuned"、
   100	        # "no gate, no resv." 等四条），手拼会让字段数多于表头、DictReader 整体错位
   101	        # （2026-09-24 审稿人抓）。csv.writer 会自动加引号。
   102	        import io as _io
   103	        buf = _io.StringIO()
   104	        _w = csv.writer(buf)
   105	        _w.writerow([_csv_str(x) for x in [
   106	            r["run"], LABEL.get(k, k), "%.4f" % w, r["conc"], "%.4f" % sl,
   107	            "" if f is None else "%.4f" % f, d, v, conc.get(k, "?"),
   108	            "1" if k in env_w else "0"]])
   109	        lines.append(buf.getvalue().rstrip("\r\n"))
   110	    body = "\n".join(out + lines) + "\n"
   111	    assert isinstance(body, str)
   112	    with open(out_p, "w") as fh:
   113	        fh.write(body)
   114	    print("  判决表 -> %s（%d 行）" % (out_p, len(lines)))
   115
